mtight">c</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.2861em"><span></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em"></span><span class="mbin">−</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em"></span><span class="mord mathbf">1</span><span class="mopen">[</span><span class="mord mathnormal">c</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em"></span><span class="mrel">=</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0359em">y</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.1514em"><span style="top:-2.55em;margin-left:-0.0359em;margin-right:0.05em"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right:0.0359em">g</span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.2861em"><span></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mclose">]</span><span class="mclose delimcenter" style="top:0em">)</span></span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em"></span><span class="mord">.</span></span></span></span></span>
<p>At the target class, the parenthesized term subtracts one from the saved
probability. Every class component is then multiplied by the same upstream and
mean scale <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mover accent="true"><mi>L</mi><mo>ˉ</mo></mover><mi mathvariant="normal">/</mi><mi>G</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\bar L/G</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1.0701em;vertical-align:-0.25em"></span><span class="mord accent"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.8201em"><span style="top:-3em"><span class="pstrut" style="height:3em"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">L</span></span><span style="top:-3.2523em"><span class="pstrut" style="height:3em"></span><span class="accent-body" style="left:-0.2222em"><span class="mord">ˉ</span></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mord">/</span><span class="mord mathnormal">G</span></span></span></span>.</p>
<p>The complete example builds gather, matmul, SiLU, displayed log-softmax, and
combined loss from the values above, then compares its forward and reverse
tensors with the fixed-array calculation. It calls <code>backward_with_trace</code>
because that comparison reads the adjoints of the SiLU output, matrix-product
output, and gathered-row output. Ordinary training uses a lean method:
<code>backward</code> when implicit graph retention is appropriate, or
<code>backward_with_seed</code> when the caller must choose retention or release. The lean
and traced methods use the same reverse calculation; tracing records its results
rather than implementing the local VJPs a second time.</p>
<figure class="rust-source" data-source-path="rust/demos/ch16-model-autodiff-ops/src/lib.rs" data-source-region="shared-model-vjp-fixture" data-astro-cid-i3uagjh5> <figcaption data-astro-cid-i3uagjh5> <span data-astro-cid-i3uagjh5>Build the repeated-token operation chain on the shared tape and compare it with the fixed calculation</span> <code dir="ltr" data-astro-cid-i3uagjh5>rust/demos/ch16-model-autodiff-ops/src/lib.rs#shared-model-vjp-fixture</code> </figcaption> <pre class="astro-code github-dark-high-contrast rust-source-code" style="background-color:#0a0c10;color:#f0f3f6; overflow-x: auto;" tabindex="0" aria-label="Rust source implementing the Chapter 16 repeated-token operation chain" dir="ltr" data-astro-cid-i3uagjh5 data-language="rust"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#BDC4CC">/// Runs gather, matmul, SiLU, stable log-softmax, and combined token loss on one</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#BDC4CC">/// repeated-token example, then checks the tape against the fixed reference.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">pub</span><span style="color:#FF9492"> fn</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF"> frozen_model_example</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">() </span><span style="color:#FF9492">-></span><span style="color:#FFB757"> Result</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="color:#FFB757">FrozenModelExample</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="color:#FFB757">TensorAutodiffError</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">    let</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> embeddings </span><span style="color:#FF9492">=</span><span style="color:#FFB757"> TensorValue</span><span style="color:#FF9492">::</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">parameter</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">tensor</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#FF9492">&#x26;</span><span style="color:#91CBFF">EMBEDDING_SHAPE</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="color:#FF9492">&#x26;</span><span style="color:#91CBFF">EMBEDDING_VALUES</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">))</span><span style="color:#FF9492">?</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">    let</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> gathered </span><span style="color:#FF9492">=</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> embeddings</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">gather_rows</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#FF9492">&#x26;</span><span style="color:#91CBFF">TOKEN_IDS</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="color:#FF9492">&#x26;</span><span style="color:#91CBFF">TOKEN_SHAPE</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">)</span><span style="color:#FF9492">?</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">    let</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> weights </span><span style="color:#FF9492">=</span><span style="color:#FFB757"> TensorValue</span><span style="color:#FF9492">::</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">parameter</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">tensor</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#FF9492">&#x26;</span><span style="color:#91CBFF">WEIGHT_SHAPE</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="color:#FF9492">&#x26;</span><span style="color:#91CBFF">WEIGHT_VALUES</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">))</span><span style="color:#FF9492">?</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">    let</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> projection_preactivations </span><span style="color:#FF9492">=</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> gathered</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">matmul</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#FF9492">&#x26;</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">weights)</span><span style="color:#FF9492">?</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">    let</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> activated </span><span style="color:#FF9492">=</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> projection_preactivations</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">silu</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">()</span><span style="color:#FF9492">?</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">    let</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> log_probabilities </span><span style="color:#FF9492">=</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> activated</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">log_softmax</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#91CBFF">CLASS_AXIS</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">)</span><span style="color:#FF9492">?</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">    let</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> loss </span><span style="color:#FF9492">=</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> activated</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">indexed_mean_nll</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#91CBFF">CLASS_AXIS</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="color:#FF9492">&#x26;</span><span style="color:#91CBFF">TARGETS</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">)</span><span style="color:#FF9492">?</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">    let</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> baseline </span><span style="color:#FF9492">=</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF"> handwritten_model_backward</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">();</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#DBB7FF">    assert_close</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(gathered</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">value</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">()</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">as_slice</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(), </span><span style="color:#FF9492">&#x26;</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">baseline</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">gathered, </span><span style="color:#91CBFF">1</span><span style="color:#FF9492">e-</span><span style="color:#91CBFF">12</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#DBB7FF">    assert_close</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        projection_preactivations</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">value</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">()</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">as_slice</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">        &#x26;</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">baseline</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">projection_preactivations,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#91CBFF">        1</span><span style="color:#FF9492">e-</span><span style="color:#91CBFF">12</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">    );</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#DBB7FF">    assert_close</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(activated</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">value</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">()</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">as_slice</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(), </span><span style="color:#FF9492">&#x26;</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">baseline</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">activated, </span><span style="color:#91CBFF">1</span><span style="color:#FF9492">e-</span><span style="color:#91CBFF">12</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#DBB7FF">    assert_close</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        log_probabilities</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">value</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">()</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">as_slice</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">        &#x26;</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">baseline</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">log_probabilities,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#91CBFF">        1</span><span style="color:#FF9492">e-</span><span style="color:#91CBFF">12</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">    );</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#DBB7FF">    assert_close</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(loss</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">value</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">()</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">as_slice</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(), </span><span style="color:#FF9492">&#x26;</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">[baseline</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">loss], </span><span style="color:#91CBFF">1</span><span style="color:#FF9492">e-</span><span style="color:#91CBFF">12</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">    let</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> backward </span><span style="color:#FF9492">=</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> loss</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">backward_with_trace</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">()</span><span style="color:#FF9492">?</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">    let</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> loss_input_gradient </span><span style="color:#FF9492">=</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF"> pass_adjoint</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#FF9492">&#x26;</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">backward, </span><span style="color:#FFB757">TensorOperation</span><span style="color:#FF9492">::</span><span style="color:#FFB757">Silu</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">    let</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> matmul_output_gradient </span><span style="color:#FF9492">=</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF"> pass_adjoint</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#FF9492">&#x26;</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">backward, </span><span style="color:#FFB757">TensorOperation</span><span style="color:#FF9492">::</span><span style="color:#FFB757">MatMul</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">    let</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> gathered_gradient </span><span style="color:#FF9492">=</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF"> pass_adjoint</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#FF9492">&#x26;</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">backward, </span><span style="color:#FFB757">TensorOperation</span><span style="color:#FF9492">::</span><span style="color:#FFB757">GatherRows</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">    let</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> embedding_gradient </span><span style="color:#FF9492">=</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> embeddings</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">        .</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">gradient_snapshot</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">        .</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">expect</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#ADDCFF">"embeddings are a parameter"</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">    let</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> weight_gradient </span><span style="color:#FF9492">=</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> weights</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">        .</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">gradient_snapshot</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">        .</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">expect</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#ADDCFF">"weights are a parameter"</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#DBB7FF">    assert_close</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        loss_input_gradient</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">as_slice</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">        &#x26;</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">baseline</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">loss_input_gradient,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#91CBFF">        1</span><span style="color:#FF9492">e-</span><span style="color:#91CBFF">12</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">    );</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#DBB7FF">    assert_close</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        matmul_output_gradient</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">as_slice</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">        &#x26;</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">baseline</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">matmul_output_gradient,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#91CBFF">        1</span><span style="color:#FF9492">e-</span><span style="color:#91CBFF">12</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">    );</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#DBB7FF">    assert_close</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        gathered_gradient</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">as_slice</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">        &#x26;</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">baseline</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">gathered_gradient,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#91CBFF">        1</span><span style="color:#FF9492">e-</span><span style="color:#91CBFF">12</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">    );</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#DBB7FF">    assert_close</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        embedding_gradient</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">as_slice</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">        &#x26;</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">baseline</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">embedding_gradient,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#91CBFF">        1</span><span style="color:#FF9492">e-</span><span style="color:#91CBFF">12</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">    );</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#DBB7FF">    assert_close</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(weight_gradient</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">as_slice</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(), </span><span style="color:#FF9492">&#x26;</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">baseline</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">weight_gradient, </span><span style="color:#91CBFF">1</span><span style="color:#FF9492">e-</span><span style="color:#91CBFF">12</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FFB757">    Ok</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#FFB757">FrozenModelExample</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        embeddings</span><span style="color:#FF9492">:</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> embeddings</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">value_snapshot</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        token_ids</span><span style="color:#FF9492">:</span><span style="color:#91CBFF"> TOKEN_IDS</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">to_vec</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        weights</span><span style="color:#FF9492">:</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> weights</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">value_snapshot</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        targets</span><span style="color:#FF9492">:</span><span style="color:#91CBFF"> TARGETS</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">to_vec</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        gathered</span><span style="color:#FF9492">:</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> gathered</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">value_snapshot</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        projection_preactivations</span><span style="color:#FF9492">:</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> projection_preactivations</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">value_snapshot</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        activated</span><span style="color:#FF9492">:</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> activated</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">value_snapshot</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        log_probabilities</span><span style="color:#FF9492">:</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> log_probabilities</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">value_snapshot</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        loss</span><span style="color:#FF9492">:</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> loss</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">value_snapshot</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        backward,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        loss_input_gradient,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        matmul_output_gradient,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        gathered_gradient,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        embedding_gradient,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        weight_gradient,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        baseline,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">    })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">}</span></span></code></pre> </figure>
<p>Each new VJP is compared with sampled central differences. The two matrix
parents are checked separately; gather, exp, log, SiLU, log-softmax, and indexed
mean NLL each get their own selected coordinates.</p>
<figure class="rust-source" data-source-path="rust/demos/ch16-model-autodiff-ops/src/lib.rs" data-source-region="model-vjp-gradchecks" data-astro-cid-i3uagjh5> <figcaption data-astro-cid-i3uagjh5> <span data-astro-cid-i3uagjh5>Compare every new local VJP with sampled central differences</span> <code dir="ltr" data-astro-cid-i3uagjh5>rust/demos/ch16-model-autodiff-ops/src/lib.rs#model-vjp-gradchecks</code> </figcaption> <pre class="astro-code github-dark-high-contrast rust-source-code" style="background-color:#0a0c10;color:#f0f3f6; overflow-x: auto;" tabindex="0" aria-label="Rust source numerically checking every Chapter 16 model-operation VJP" dir="ltr" data-astro-cid-i3uagjh5 data-language="rust"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#BDC4CC">/// Checks both matmul parents plus gather, exp, log, SiLU, log-softmax, and NLL.</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#DBB7FF">        tensor</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#FF9492">&#x26;</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">[</span><span style="color:#91CBFF">2</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="color:#91CBFF">3</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">], </span><span style="color:#FF9492">&#x26;</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">[</span><span style="color:#91CBFF">0.7</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="color:#FF9492">-</span><span style="color:#91CBFF">0.4</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="color:#91CBFF">1.1</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="color:#FF9492">-</span><span style="color:#91CBFF">0.2</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="color:#91CBFF">0.3</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="color:#91CBFF">0.8</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">]),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">        move</span><span style="color:#FF9492"> |</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">parameter</span><span style="color:#FF9492">|</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">            let</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> weights </span><span style="color:#FF9492">=</span><span style="color:#FFB757"> TensorValue</span><span style="color:#FF9492">::</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">constant</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(weights_for_tape)</span><span style="color:#FF9492">?</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#DBB7FF">            sum_to_scalar</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(parameter</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">log_softmax</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#91CBFF">1</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">)</span><span style="color:#FF9492">?.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">mul</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#FF9492">&#x26;</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">weights)</span><span style="color:#FF9492">?</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">        move</span><span style="color:#FF9492"> |</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">candidate</span><span style="color:#FF9492">|</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">            let</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> output </span><span style="color:#FF9492">=</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF"> tensor_log_softmax</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#FF9492">&#x26;</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">candidate</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">view</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(), </span><span style="color:#91CBFF">1</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">                .</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">expect</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#ADDCFF">"the worked probability axis is valid"</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#DBB7FF">            weighted_sum</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(output</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">as_slice</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(), weights_for_objective</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">as_slice</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">())</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">    )</span><span style="color:#FF9492">?</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">    let</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> indexed_mean_nll </span><span style="color:#FF9492">=</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF"> sampled_model_check</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#DBB7FF">        tensor</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#FF9492">&#x26;</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">[</span><span style="color:#91CBFF">2</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="color:#91CBFF">3</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">], </span><span style="color:#FF9492">&#x26;</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">[</span><span style="color:#91CBFF">0.7</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="color:#FF9492">-</span><span style="color:#91CBFF">0.4</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="color:#91CBFF">1.1</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="color:#FF9492">-</span><span style="color:#91CBFF">0.2</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="color:#91CBFF">0.3</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="color:#91CBFF">0.8</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">]),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">        |</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">parameter</span><span style="color:#FF9492">|</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> parameter</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">indexed_mean_nll</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#91CBFF">1</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="color:#FF9492">&#x26;</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">[</span><span style="color:#91CBFF">2</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="color:#91CBFF">0</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">]),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">        |</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">candidate</span><span style="color:#FF9492">|</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#DBB7FF">            tensor_indexed_mean_nll</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#FF9492">&#x26;</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">candidate</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">view</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(), </span><span style="color:#91CBFF">1</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="color:#FF9492">&#x26;</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">[</span><span style="color:#91CBFF">2</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="color:#91CBFF">0</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">])</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">    )</span><span style="color:#FF9492">?</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">;</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">    let</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> checks </span><span style="color:#FF9492">=</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF"> vec!</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">[</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">            operation</span><span style="color:#FF9492">:</span><span style="color:#ADDCFF"> "matmul-left"</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>

<p>Invalid token IDs, invalid target classes, empty target sets, and exponential
overflow are rejected without changing previously recorded gradients. The
example snapshots the affected gradient immediately before and after each
failure rather than relying only on an initially zero state:</p>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        invalid_id_before </span><span style="color:#FF9492">==</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF"> tensor_bits</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#FF9492">&#x26;</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">table</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">gradient</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">()</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">expect</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#ADDCFF">"table gradient remains"</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">));</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">    let</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> invalid_target_before </span><span style="color:#FF9492">=</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF"> tensor_bits</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#FF9492">&#x26;</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">logits</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">gradient</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">()</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">expect</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#ADDCFF">"logit gradient exists"</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">));</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">    let</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> invalid_target </span><span style="color:#FF9492">=</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> logits</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">        .</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">indexed_mean_nll</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#91CBFF">1</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="color:#FF9492">&#x26;</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">[</span><span style="color:#91CBFF">0</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="color:#91CBFF">2</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">])</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">        .</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">expect_err</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#ADDCFF">"class two is outside a two-class row"</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">    let</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> invalid_target_gradient_unchanged </span><span style="color:#FF9492">=</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        invalid_target_before </span><span style="color:#FF9492">==</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF"> tensor_bits</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#FF9492">&#x26;</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">logits</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">gradient</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">()</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">expect</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#ADDCFF">"logit gradient remains"</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">));</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">    let</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> empty_targets_before </span><span style="color:#FF9492">=</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#DBB7FF">        tensor_bits</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#FF9492">&#x26;</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">empty_logits</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">gradient</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">()</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">expect</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#ADDCFF">"empty gradient exists"</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">));</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">    let</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> empty_targets </span><span style="color:#FF9492">=</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> empty_logits</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">        .</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">indexed_mean_nll</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#91CBFF">1</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">, </span><span style="color:#FF9492">&#x26;</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">[])</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">        .</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">expect_err</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#ADDCFF">"a mean over no targets is undefined"</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">    let</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> empty_targets_gradient_unchanged </span><span style="color:#FF9492">=</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> empty_targets_before</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">        ==</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF"> tensor_bits</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#FF9492">&#x26;</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">empty_logits</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">gradient</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">()</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">expect</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#ADDCFF">"empty gradient remains"</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">));</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">    let</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> exp_overflow_before </span><span style="color:#FF9492">=</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF"> tensor_bits</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#FF9492">&#x26;</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">overflow</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">gradient</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">()</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">expect</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#ADDCFF">"scalar gradient exists"</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">));</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">    let</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> exp_overflow </span><span style="color:#FF9492">=</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> overflow</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">        .</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">exp</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">        .</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">expect_err</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#ADDCFF">"the finite-forward invariant rejects positive infinity"</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">    let</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> exp_overflow_gradient_unchanged </span><span style="color:#FF9492">=</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        exp_overflow_before </span><span style="color:#FF9492">==</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF"> tensor_bits</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#FF9492">&#x26;</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">overflow</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">gradient</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">()</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">expect</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#ADDCFF">"scalar gradient remains"</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">));</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">    let</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> gradients_unchanged </span><span style="color:#FF9492">=</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> invalid_id_gradient_unchanged</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">        &#x26;&#x26;</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> invalid_target_gradient_unchanged</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">        &#x26;&#x26;</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> empty_targets_gradient_unchanged</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">        &#x26;&#x26;</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> exp_overflow_gradient_unchanged;</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FFB757">    Ok</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#FFB757">ModelErrorExample</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        invalid_id,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        invalid_target,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        empty_targets,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        exp_overflow,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        invalid_id_gradient_unchanged,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        invalid_target_gradient_unchanged,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        empty_targets_gradient_unchanged,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        exp_overflow_gradient_unchanged,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        gradients_unchanged,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">    })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">}</span></span></code></pre> </figure>
<p>The executable prints the worked values, the three scalar derivatives, the
sampled central-difference result, and the aggregate error-preservation result:</p>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">    );</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">    );</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">    );</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#DBB7FF">        tensor_text</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#FF9492">&#x26;</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">example</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">weight_gradient)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">    );</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#DBB7FF">    println!</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#ADDCFF">        "scalar probes: exp(0)->({}, {}) | log(1)->({}, {}) | silu(0)->({}, {})"</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#DBB7FF">        fixed</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(probes[</span><span style="color:#91CBFF">0</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">]</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">output),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#DBB7FF">        fixed</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(probes[</span><span style="color:#91CBFF">0</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">]</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">gradient),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#DBB7FF">        fixed</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(probes[</span><span style="color:#91CBFF">1</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">]</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">output),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#DBB7FF">        fixed</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(probes[</span><span style="color:#91CBFF">1</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">]</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">gradient),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#DBB7FF">        fixed</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(probes[</span><span style="color:#91CBFF">2</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">]</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">output),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#DBB7FF">        fixed</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(probes[</span><span style="color:#91CBFF">2</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">]</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">gradient),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">    );</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#DBB7FF">    println!</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#ADDCFF">        "gradcheck: {}; pass={}"</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        gradchecks</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">            .</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">checks</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">            .</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">iter</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">            .</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">map</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#FF9492">|</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">check</span><span style="color:#FF9492">|</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6"> check</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">operation)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">            .</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">collect</span><span style="color:#FF9492">::</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;</span><span style="color:#FFB757">Vec</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">&#x3C;_>>()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF9492">            .</span><span style="color:#DBB7FF">join</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#ADDCFF">" | "</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        gradchecks</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">passed</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">    );</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#DBB7FF">    println!</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#ADDCFF">        "typed errors: invalid-id | invalid-target | empty-targets | exp-overflow; gradients unchanged={}"</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">        errors</span><span style="color:#FF9492">.</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">gradients_unchanged</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F0F3F6">    );</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#DBB7FF">    println!</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">(</span><span style="color:#ADDCFF">"chapter 17 handoff: initialize trainable values reproducibly"</span><span style="color:#F0F3F6">);</span></span></code></pre> </figure>

<h2 id="follow-four-occurrence-gradients-into-three-parameter-rows">Follow four occurrence gradients into three parameter rows</h2>
<p>The figure separates the compact forward chain from reverse accumulation. The
fork after SiLU is explicit: log-softmax and combined mean NLL are separate
calls over the same activated logits, and each retains the probabilities from
its own checked forward call. Target rows expose the negative and positive
derivatives, their zero class sum, and the exact parent-gradient shapes through
SiLU and both matrix operands.</p>
<p>The destination-row boxes contain their own occurrence contributions. Row <code>1</code>
contains positions <code>0</code>, <code>1</code>, and <code>2</code> before their sum; row <code>2</code> contains position
<code>3</code>; row <code>0</code> is visibly unused. That arrangement makes it possible to see why
four materialized output rows produce only three parent-table gradient rows.</p>
<figure class="course-diagram model-autodiff-ops-diagram" data-diagram-style="course-v1" data-visualization-id="model-autodiff-ops" tabindex="0" aria-labelledby="model-autodiff-ops-lesson-title" aria-describedby="model-autodiff-ops-lesson-description" data-astro-cid-iyx2ocya> <figcaption class="course-diagram__caption" data-astro-cid-iyx2ocya> <h3 id="model-autodiff-ops-lesson-title" data-astro-cid-iyx2ocya>Follow a repeated token ID from lookup to loss and back</h3> <p id="model-autodiff-ops-lesson-description" class="course-diagram__description" data-astro-cid-iyx2ocya>Inspect the compact forward chain, signed target gradients with zero class sums, both matrix VJPs, and four occurrence contributions grouped inside three destination embedding rows.</p> </figcaption> <dl <h2 id="predict-before-running-rust">Predict before running Rust</h2>
<ol>
<li>Map flat positions <code>0</code> through <code>3</code> to <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mo class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em"></span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord mathnormal">b</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">t</span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span> for <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">B=1</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0502em">B</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em"></span><span class="mrel">=</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em"></span><span class="mord">1</span></span></span></span> and <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>T</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">T=4</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.1389em">T</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em"></span><span class="mrel">=</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em"></span><span class="mord">4</span></span></span></span>.</li>
<li>Write all four gathered rows, projection-preactivation rows, loss-logit rows, log-probability rows, and the scalar mean loss.</li>
<li>Predict why the correct-target gradient is negative, the competing gradient is positive, and each two-class row sums to zero.</li>
<li>Apply the SiLU derivative at zero. What is the gradient entering matmul at each position?</li>
<li>Predict the shapes of both matmul parent gradients and calculate <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>W</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">dW</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6944em"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">d</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.1389em">W</span></span></span></span>.</li>
<li>List the three contributions to embedding row <code>1</code> before adding them. What reaches rows <code>0</code> and <code>2</code>?</li>
<li>Predict both value and local derivative for <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>exp</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mn>0</mn><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\exp(0)</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em"></span><span class="mop">exp</span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord">0</span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span>, <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>ln</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\ln(1)</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em"></span><span class="mop">ln</span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord">1</span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span>, and <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">SiLU</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mn>0</mn><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\operatorname{SiLU}(0)</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em"></span><span class="mop"><span class="mord mathrm">SiLU</span></span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord">0</span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span>.</li>
<li>Explain why logits near <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mo>±</mo><mn>1000</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\pm1000</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.7278em;vertical-align:-0.0833em"></span><span class="mord">±</span><span class="mord">1000</span></span></span></span> require maximum-shifted normalization and why each autodiff probability operation saves the probabilities emitted by its own checked forward call. Does “one call” mean one read of each logit? In what sense are the saved <code>f64</code> values identical, and do the two probability branches share a result?</li>
<li>Which row-gather facts are checked at the public entry, and which work may the validated plan omit? At what stage are an invalid target class and an empty target set detected?</li>
<li>Misconception check: should row <code>1</code> be divided by three after its three occurrence gradients are added?</li>
</ol>

<li>The map is <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>↦</mo><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mn>0</mn><mo <li>Gather gives <code>[1,-1]</code> three times and <code>[-1,1]</code> once. <li>Increasing the selected target logit reduces NLL, so its derivative is negative. Increasing a competitor raises its probability at the target’s expense, so its derivative is positive. Softmax probabilities sum to one and the one-hot target also sums to one, leaving class-row gradient sum zero.</li>
<li>SiLU has derivative <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">/</mi><mn>2</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">1/2</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em"></span><span class="mord">1/2</span></span></span></span> at zero. The first three rows become <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">[</mo><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">/</mi><mn>16</mn><mo separator="true">,</mo><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">/</mi><mn>16</mn><mo stretchy="false">]</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">[-1/16,1/16]</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em"></span><span class="mopen">[</span><span class="mord">−</span><span class="mord">1/16</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em"></span><span class="mord">1/16</span><span class="mclose">]</span></span></span></span>; the last becomes <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">[</mo><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">/</mi><mn>16</mn><mo separator="true">,</mo><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">/</mi><mn>16</mn><mo stretchy="false">]</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">[1/16,-1/16]</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em"></span><span class="mopen">[</span><span class="mord">1/16</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em"></span><span class="mord">−</span><span class="mord">1/16</span><span class="mclose">]</span></span></span></span>.</li>
<li>The left-parent gradient has gathered shape <code>[4,2]</code>; the right-parent gradient has weight shape <code>[2,2]</code>. The value is <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>W</mi><mo>=</mo><mo stretchy="false">[</mo><mo stretchy="false">[</mo><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">/</mi><mn>4</mn><mo separator="true">,</mo><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">/</mi><mn>4</mn><mo stretchy="false">]</mo><mo separator="true">,</mo><mo stretchy="false">[</mo><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">/</mi><mn>4</mn><mo separator="true">,</mo><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">/</mi><mn>4</mn><mo stretchy="false">]</mo><mo stretchy="false">]</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">dW=[[-1/4,1/4],[1/4,-1/4]]</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6944em"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">d</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.1389em">W</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em"></span><span class="mrel">=</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em"></span><span class="mopen">[[</span><span class="mord">−</span><span class="mord">1/4</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em"></span><span class="mord">1/4</span><span class="mclose">]</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em"></span><span class="mopen">[</span><span class="mord">1/4</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em"></span><span class="mord">−</span><span class="mord">1/4</span><span class="mclose">]]</span></span></span></span>.</li>
<li>Positions <code>0</code>, <code>1</code>, and <code>2</code> each contribute <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">[</mo><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">/</mi><mn>8</mn><mo separator="true">,</mo><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">/</mi><mn>8</mn><mo stretchy="false">]</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">[-1/8,-1/8]</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em"></span><span class="mopen">[</span><span class="mord">−</span><span class="mord">1/8</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em"></span><span class="mord">−</span><span class="mord">1/8</span><span class="mclose">]</span></span></span></span> to row <code>1</code>, so it gets <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">[</mo><mo>−</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">/</mi><mn>8</mn><mo separator="true">,</mo><mo>−</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">/</mi><mn>8</mn><mo stretchy="false">]</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">[-3/8,-3/8]</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em"></span><span class="mopen">[</span><span class="mord">−</span><span class="mord">3/8</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em"></span><span class="mord">−</span><span class="mord">3/8</span><span class="mclose">]</span></span></span></span>. Row <code>2</code> gets <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">[</mo><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">/</mi><mn>8</mn><mo separator="true">,</mo><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">/</mi><mn>8</mn><mo stretchy="false">]</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">[1/8,1/8]</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em"></span><span class="mopen">[</span><span class="mord">1/8</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em"></span><span class="mord">1/8</span><span class="mclose">]</span></span></span></span>; unused row <code>0</code> gets <code>[0,0]</code>.</li>
<li><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>exp</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mn>0</mn><mo stretchy="false">)</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\exp(0)=1</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em"></span><span class="mop">exp</span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord">0</span><span class="mclose">)</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em"></span><span class="mrel">=</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em"></span><span class="mord">1</span></span></span></span> with derivative <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">1</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em"></span><span class="mord">1</span></span></span></span>; <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>ln</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo stretchy="false">)</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\ln(1)=0</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em"></span><span class="mop">ln</span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord">1</span><span class="mclose">)</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em"></span><span class="mrel">=</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em"></span><span class="mord">0</span></span></span></span> with derivative <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">1</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em"></span><span class="mord">1</span></span></span></span>; <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">SiLU</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mn>0</mn><mo stretchy="false">)</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\operatorname{SiLU}(0)=0</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em"></span><span class="mop"><span class="mord mathrm">SiLU</span></span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord">0</span><span class="mclose">)</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em"></span><span class="mrel">=</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em"></span><span class="mord">0</span></span></span></span> with derivative <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">/</mi><mn>2</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">1/2</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em"></span><span class="mord">1/2</span></span></span></span>.</li>
<li>Maximum shifting prevents avoidable overflow without changing the distribution. Each log-softmax or indexed-mean-NLL operation emits its primary result and the probability values needed by its VJP in one checked call. The saved tensor contains the same emitted <code>f64</code> values, bit for bit; this is identity of stored floating-point values, not exact real arithmetic. Backward uses those values without another <code>softmax</code> call. “One call” does not mean one logit read: the preliminary scan checks finiteness, <code>RowStats</code> uses separate class scans for the maximum and shifted-exponential sum, and the emitter writes the probability output. The lesson’s two branches are separate operations with separate calls and saved tensors; they share only the input logits.</li>
<li>The public row-gather entry first checks operand availability, table rank, the logical selector shape, the selector count, and then IDs in flat order. Its validated plan lets row copying and scatter-add reuse those facts without rescanning the raw request. The indexed-loss entry validates the class axis, checks that the target count equals the number of class-axis groups, rejects zero groups, and then checks targets in flat group order before computing the loss or saved probabilities.</li>
<li>No. Every contribution already includes the loss’s <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">/</mi><mn>4</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">1/4</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em"></span><span class="mord">1/4</span></span></span></span> factor. Gather reversal sums the three contributions exactly once and does not apply another mean.</li>
</ol></details>

<h2 id="initialize-the-values-these-gradients-will-train">Initialize the values these gradients will train</h2>
<p>The cumulative implementation can now differentiate a compact chain from
selected embedding rows through a projection, nonlinearity, and stable mean
token loss. This is an operation-level example, not the final decoder architecture:
later feed-forward blocks use SiLU internally, and a separate vocabulary
projection produces the decoder’s loss logits.</p>
<p>Later chapters will package these operations into embedding, linear,
normalization, SwiGLU, and attention layers. Correct gradients still do not
choose useful parameter values. The next problem is choosing initial values
that break symmetry without making signals vanish or explode, so Chapter 17
adds deterministic, scale-aware initialization without adding another VJP.</p> </div> <nav class="chapter-navigation" aria-label="Chapter navigation" data-chapter-navigation data-astro-cid-44eis63d>
